   The most dramatic sighting of an
 Wlidentified flying object (UFO) to be
 reported in this part of Mississippi took
 place near Jayess Saturday, February
 5, and was seen at close quarters by at
 least six adults.
    Another sighting was reported in
 Madison CoWlty Wednesday, February
 3, and was seen there by a constable
 and a deputy sheriff.
    The Wednesday sighting was
 reported by the associated press and on
 local television news programs from
 Jackson.
    One version of the Saturday sighting
 at Jayess, reported by the Brookhaven
 Leader-Times, was confirmed by
 another Jayess resident in a teleohone
 interview with the editor of The
 Tylertown Times SWtday.
    MRS. DARWIN Alexander, who said
 she had never seen anything like the
 object, said that she and her husband
 both witnessed the sighting.
    "We looked out our patio window

    Was it a craft from outer space, the 200-foot-

    "I am as positive of what I saw as I can be of

    But UFO investigators Jerry Phillips and Bob
 Gribble have a more down-to-Earth explanation.                  (8) VFA (Very Flashy Airplane)
    Phillips is an Everett police dispatcher who's
 studied UFOs for the past three years. He en-
 courages witnesses to report their sightings to
 him at 252-5037.
    Gribble is a retired Seattle fire figllter who
 has tracked down flying saucer reports since
 1955. He runs the UFO Reporting Center from
 his home in southeast Seattle. It's a paneled up-
 stairs office crammed with maps, card files and
 red "hotline" telephones to which, by dialing
 722-3000, people can report UFO sightings 24
 hours a day.
    After reading of the Gates' experience and re-
 ceiving other reports, Phillips and Gribble sus-
 pected. the -witnesses had seen something that's
 inspired scores of slinilar UFO sightings - an
 advertising aircraft flown out of Renton airport
 and owned by Melvin Reynolds of Seattle.
    The plane is a sin~le-engine Cessna 150. It is
 strung from wingtip to wingtip beneath its fuse-
 lage with a grid of 315 lights. The lights spell
 out messages which · can be read only by per-
 sons directly underneath the plane. Seen from
 an angle, it is frequently mistaken for a UFO.
    PhilUPs, who might well have received the re-
 ports himself had he not been hospitalized Oct.
 18, checked with Paine Field. Although the tow-
 er _ ha~ not remembered the incideat when que-
 ried ~ this newspaper, air traffic controllers
 recalled that the Remolds plane had flown over
 Everett between 8:30. and 9:30 that night.
   Contacted by Phillips, Reynolds also remem-
 bered ;the Oct. 18 flight : He said he flew over

 ish Riirer, _then in a counterclockwise loop over
 Port Garner Bay and downtown Everett and fi-
 nally south above Interstate 5, west over Casino

         AF test cloud                                                                                  STAR
                                                                                                        ANNISTON, AL
         l(ioked.like UFO                                                                               DEC 6, 1976


             The colorful , shimmering cloud seen Wednesday night in                   south of Eglin Air Force Base. It measured about 100 miles
           the sky south of Anniston was not a UFO attack from outer                   across and was seen in Anniston as about the size of a hand
           space. It was a U.S. Air Force atmospheric test.                            held at arm's length.
             According to the Air Force, the cloud was made up of ionic                  Air Force spokesmen said the test is part of a four-month
           barium gas , released into the ionosphere by a rocket fired                ·project to evaluate national defense-oriented satellite
           from Eglin Air Force Base in Florida Wednesday.                             communication links through an artificially disturbed
             Communications officers at the air base said the cloua,                   ionosphere.
           which appeared shortly after sunset, was illuminated by the                   There will be six more tests in the project, the Air Force
           sun against a darkened sky and caused.residents of Anniston                 said. Similar tests were conducted at the Florida Panhandle
           and towns all across the southeast to call newspapers, police               air base in 1971 and in the late 1960s.
           departments and sheriffs.                                                     A base spokesman said the cloud posed no danger to the

                                                                      incessantly. It was Britain's tyranny that drew them to-

~ 1)  City and country of incident: La Spezia, Italy
   2) Date of incident: 9/7/76
   3) Name of paper and date of clip: La Razon- 9/8/76
   4) City and country of newspaper: Buenos Aires, Argentina

   ITALY'S EXTRATERRESTRIAL COULD BE A CIRCUS DWARF
   La Spezia (ANSA) - The "extraterrestrial" seen yesterday on a hill behind
   La Spezia and photographed by some members of the ORU (Organization for
   the Investigation of UFOs), could be a dwarf escaped from a circus that
   performed some days ago in this cit~. This is the latest news circulat-
   ing in the city, after some "ufologists" reported to police having seen
   over Mount Parodi, an extraterrestrial, approx. 1.20m tall, thin, wear-
   ing an opaque overall with a cloak that completely covered his face and
   who walked in an unmistakable way, as if he slided on the ground. The
   ufologists even delivered the photographs which"are original -stated the
   chief of police- i.e., they are not a photographic setup. But from there
   to sea.ng an extraterrestrial there is quite a difference".
   Immediately after receiving the information the polic~ade enquiries
   without any success. It is now investigating to see if effectively the
   "extraterrestrial being" is a little man escaped from the circus of
   Nando~ Liana and Rinaldo Orfei, which abandoned La Spezia some days ago.
   In the same area of Mt.Parodi some people claimed having seen flying
   objects previously.

~1)   City and country of incident: United States
   2) Date of incident: -
   3) Name of paper and date of clip: La Razon -Oct. 3. 1976
   4) City and country of newspaper: Buenos Aires, Argentina

   Mexico (ANSA) -The NA.SA of the U.S. admitted now that "in each of the
   manned American spaceflights there has been a strange companion which
   was none of the carrier rocket's elements, but unidentified flying objects
   of which they have film and photographs, revealed the popular radio and
   TV commentator Pedro Ferriz. "With this -said the man who was present
   at all U.S. launchings and has described them for the Spanish speaking
   TV public- the skepticism about UFOs has ended". Ferriz considered that
   "most people think we are being visited by beings from other worlds"
   and proceeded to cite polls made by the Gallup Agency in the U.S.,
   according to which "52% of Americans share this belief".
   As Director for several years of a TV program ("Un Mundo Nos Vigila-A
   World is Watching Us") Ferriz took part in the first national symposium
   on UFOs held in this city, an.d thus could express his opinion that we
   are receiving extraterrestrial visits, but was skeptical of those
   participants who assured having traveled to other planets and who "are
   in contact with extraterrestrial beings". The symposium days have been
   long and heated, and they were attended by scientists as well as "seers"
   and "illuminated ones". Some statements promoted discussions and others
   laughter among some 500 attendants.

   IT WAS DISCLOSED THAT 20,000 CANNON SHOTS WERE AIMED AGAINST THREE     UFOs
   THAT FLEW OVER LOS ANGELES IN 1944.
   There are 20,000 photographs,   including those taken during flights   to
   the Moon.
   M€xico (ANSA) - Dr. Dennis Huck, of the Center of UFO Studies of Evanston,
   Ill., presented here today at the First Mexican Symposium on UFOs some
   200 UFO slides "perfectly verified", one of them taken from Apollo XI
   during its trip to the Moon, by astronauts Neil Armstrong, Michaa
   Collins and Edwin Aldrin in July 1969. With these testimonies, a part of
   the thousands of photos that the most important center in the world has,
   to study UFOs, obtained with complex and expensive equipments which
   include radar, very sensitive cameras, Lasers, telescopes and even


                                                                                 ®
    computers, "it is evident we are being visited by extraterrestrial
    beings". In the photos, taken in several countries, by da1 and by
    night, different types of UFOs can be seen, isolated or in formation,
    one of them emerging from the water, most of them in the shape of
    "saucers", although with variations according to "sighting" dates, as
    if they had developed towards more aerodynamic forms. Huck, a close
    aide of Dr. J.Allen Hynek, the first "ufologist" of the world, calls
    the attention to a photograph taken in 1944 over the city of Los Ange-
    les, Cal.Three UFOs are seen over the contour of the buildings and the
    sky   swept by searchlights, and he tells that at that time aeroguns of
    the American Army aimed 20,000 shots against the objects, and the city
    was besiegued and without lights for five hours. He also states that
    the Center for UFO Studies of Evanston received more than 100 reports
    of "kidnappings by extraterrestrials, even though only three have been
    verified; in which sufficient evidence was found and the witnesses were
    interrogated with the aid of hypnosis, drugs and lie detectors". How-
    ever, he recommended that "the feet be kept upon the ground and all
    reports be very well investigated", of people who claim they meet with
    extraterrestrial beings. Those who claim having travelled to other pla-
    nets or to have contacts with extraterrestrial beings have presented
    no proof, said Dr. Guillermo Hesselbach today, and called them
    charlatans.

                                         Finnish and Swedish Lapland
    2)    Date of incidents: First fortnight of November 1976
    3)    Name of paper and date of clip: Cr<1nica - Nov. 18, 1976

    (1)    UFOs Invade Earth South and North -                The Andes,      a safe landing place.

    Santiago, Chile, 17 (ANSA) - According to a Chilean student of UFOs,
    these are obliged to pass over this country to enter Earth. The state-
    ment belongs to Gerardo Ojeda, a researcher who has dedicated nine
    years to collect data on these phenomena. Ojeda claims that "it has
    been verified that the entrance of the saucers is through the polar
    caps. Thus they enter thru the Anctartic, and as when arriving to
    Earch they need a place that offers the best possibility of not being
    seen~ they land in that which geographically allows them to stop and
    hide. And the Andes range offers such guarantees."
    He explains that the reason for these "invasions" is to observe, and
    forecasts that next year, between September and December, appearances
    will be more frequent, to decrease in subsequent years. Ojeda has pre-
    pared a file with 7,000 cards on UFO sightings in all the world,
    press clippings and data exchanged with other countries. According to
    him, there are three kinds of UFOs: first the "flying cigar", of
    cylindrical shape, with sharpened points, three hundred meters long;
    second, the "mattEr ship" which keeps flying discs inside, and last
    the "Adamsky" type which is an inverted soup plate. Ojeda ends by
    stating the necessity that governments should take the decision of
    carrying out a thorough investigation of the phenomenon.


    (2)   A Laplander      Attacked     with a Ray.
    S t o c k holm s 1 7 - ( F r an c e P r e s s e ) - A Finn i s h Lap 1 an de r c 1 aimed h a vi n g
    been the victim of a UFO seen more than a week ago in several places
    of Finnish and Swedish Lapland.
    This was stated today by the newspaper "Haparanda-Bladet". It informed
    that said Laplander, Eero Lammi, 19, who lives in Uleaaberg (Finland),
    still has in his chest reddish traces made by a "luminous ray" emerg-
    ing from two sparkling spheres which moved on a level with the ground.
    Lammi approached the artifact out of curiosity, being immediately
    wounded by the mysterious ray and thrown down. This phenomenon was
    observed by some of his friends who, less courageous than he, remained
    at a safe distance.
    Another paper, the "Norrobttens-Kuriren" informed that five youths
    from Kalix in Sweden, sighted the same phenomenon Monday night. The
    paper sent a photo reporter to the place and negatives taken by him
    show that in the place where the UFO was, the grass frozen by the winter
    was flattened in a circular area of 5 meters diameter. In the middle
    the grass is completely calcined, while around it the traces of eight
    small circles can be seen.
    The Swedish Army, according to the papers,                    undertook an investigation,
    but refused to make any statement.

     UFOS - NOW ENGLAND
    London - 19 (DPA)   A UFO, shaped like a cigar, with a crew dressed in
    silvery spacesuits was seen in the English country by a peasant and a
    friend, who was not yet able to forget the eyes. of one of the extra-
    terrestrials. The peasant, Ted Pratt, 58,and his companion Joyce
    Bowles~ 42, said they got quite scared when they noticed the car they
    were traveling in was vibrating strangely, then seemed to lose contact
    with the asphalt, and shortly thereafter came to a halt.
    Then -as can be read in the interview published today by "The Sunn-
    they saw a man emerging from the UFO, very well formed, who approach-
    ed the car making a strange noise. It was then when the extraterrestrial
    looked at us through the window with his horrible rose-colored eyes
    (poor guy, maybe he only was an albino ••. - J.T.)
    In spite of the great scare, Joyce Bowles tells how after a few moments
    a deep feeling of serenity took hold of her. Shortly afterwards, the
    strange visitor from other worlds returned to the spaceship.

    UFO - RESIDENTS OF METAN SAW IT AND A PLANE WANTED TO INTERCEPT IT

    Salta -Many people saw a strange object fly over the city of Metan. It
    ¥ad the shape of a spinning top, was silvery colored and stood still
     or quite a while at a height of about 2 to 3,000 meters. The UFO was
    first sighted in the early hours of the morning but then reappeared at
    around 9, to stay in the same place until noon, to the general curiosity
    of the Metan residents.
    The police confirmed the UFO's presence and expressed that as from 9 am
    it could be observed how it remained motionless in the sky, at an
    altitude of some 2,500 meters. From the Metan airport the Provincial
    Aeronautical Office tried to send a plane up to observe the strange
    object from close by, but the unfavorable atmospheric conditions pre-
    vented it. The paper "El Tribuna" had the same intention of sending up a
    plane with reporters to photograph the UFO, but the machine could not
    take off.

    URUGUAY: SHOWERS OF UFOS

    Montevideo, 2 (DPA) - The Center for the Investigation of Unidentified
    Flying Objects  of Uruguay  is currently investigating a shower of
    reports on the appearance of UF~ in the country during these last days.
    According to a spokesman of said entity, in a short period a total of
    37 cases were computed. The directors of CIOVI inform that the rhythm
    of reports is giddy and that they are doing their best to cover all of
    them in the shortest possible time. According to that Center, the most
    important cases investigated are those observed by rural workers
    in the ranch La Suiza, in Peralta, Dept. of Tacuaremb6, some 400 km
    from Montevideo. A similar investigation is being carried out of
    appearances recorded at the end of September last in the Dept. of
    Artigas.

     THE APPEARANCE OF A STATIC FLYING OBJECT ALARMS A TOWN IN SANTA FE

     Santa Fe -The town of Villa Ocampo, Dept. of General Obligado, some
     400 km NW of this city, was convulsed this morning by the mysterious
     presence of a flying object in the shape of a star, and which caused
     the most varied and whimsical comments. The presence of the object was
     noticed in the early hours of today and after 9 am it was still static,
     to the amazement and bewilderment of the neighbors who made all kinds
     of comments. In that sense they did not discard the possibility that a
     reconaissance flight be ordered from the Reconquista Air Force Base to
     the site where the UFO was.

     HALF PORTUGAL SAW A UFO

     Lisbon, 29 (France Presse) - A UFO was seen the night of Tuesday to
     Wednesday by several thousands of people in different places of
     Portugal, it was learned here today.
     The object, green and with an "orange" trail, apparently moving from
     North to South at great speed and very low altitude, was first seen
     in Covilha, 200 km NE of Lisbon, and in several places of the capital
     and its vicinity.
     A pilot of the Portuguese airline (TAP)     had previously pointed it out
     to the airport's control tower.

     THE GREAT NONSENSE

     Lima (ANSA)    A woman and her two months old suckling daughter have
     alledegly been kidnapped by a UFO in the province of Cajabamba of the
     Dept. of Cajamarca, the country's Northern Sierra. The version was
     provided to the newspaper "La Prensa" by some local peasants, who
     claimed having been witnesses to it together with five other workers.
     According to the story, the mysterious vessel in the shape of a saucer
     and a blinding violet color, appeared suddenly when the group of peasants
     of which Candelaria Tucto Chilon and her daugher (the alleged kid~apees),
     were getting ready to return to their homes.

     CARTER SAW UFOS
     Mexico, D.F. (AP) - Allen Hynek, investigator of UFOs, believes the
     American government will reactivate research on this subject, because, he
     said, President Carter is a witness to the existence of UFOs. Dr. Hynek,
     President of the Center for UFO Studies, in Illinois, came to coordinate
     the arrangements for the first international congress on UFOs which will
     be held in April in Acapulco.

     A DEFORMED REFRIGERATOR, DISCHARGED BATTERIES AND BURST BOTTLES WAS THE
     CONSEQUENCE OF THE APPEARANCE OF A UFO IN URUGUAY

     Montevideo (ANSA) - To the successive appearances in several points of
     Uruguay of UFOs, a new and impacting instance was added when it was re-
     ported that the ''presence'' of one of those mysterious objects deformed
     the metallic structure of a refrigerator, discharged the batteries of
     three cars, made a bottle with a refreshment burst and caused the logical
     surprise and commotion in the city of Paso de los Toros, some 200 km
     North of here.  The unusual event -which adds to a series that took place
     in the last weeks in the Northern and Central part of the Republic- was
     revealed to the press today by CITOVNI (one of the private local organi-
     zations that study these phenomena), who did not hesitate to state that
     they have before them one of the most complex cases of the last times.
     Residents of the region, on their part, claim to having seen not "flying
     saucers" but "strange lights:.. A spokesman for CITOVNI expressed that in
     the last ten days similar cases ocurred at Paso de los Taros almost
     daily, as is registered in the information provided by the local police.
     "The most spectacular -it said- is the case of a neighbor whose refri-
     gerator was completely bent due to the influence of the unidentified
     object."
     The witness, according to a recording . submitted by the institution, de-
     fined it as an "enormous mercury lamp, very luminous, silver-colored".

     WHAT A SCARE!      A UFO ACCOMPANIED TWO COUPLES

     Huelva, Spain, 23 (EFE) - Two couples that traveled in a car from this
     Southern Spanish city to Madrid, claimed today that a UFO accompanied
     them for ten minutes.
     At a given time, Jose Mart1nez Oliva, owner of the vehicle in which he
     traveled with his wife and another couple~ tells that a strange orange
     luminous object, in the shape of a flattened ball some 30m diameter,
     and from which issued a strong light, leveled off with the car he drove
     and accompanied him for ten minutes.
     J.Martinez finally states that the most curious thing was that on reach-
     ing Madrid in the early hours of the morning, they learned from a paper
     about the report made by a pilot of Iberia airlines, who told how from
     the Galician city of Santiago de Compostela (North of Spain) to Madrid a
     strange object accompanied him, of very similar characteristics to that
     seen by him and his companions during this trip.

     FOUR SUNS

     MOSCOW, 29 (AFP) - Four suns shone at the same time on October 25th in
     the sky of a city in Northern Siberia, disclosed the paper "Socialist
     Industry".
     According to the paper, this PEnomenon indicates a forthcoming tempe-
     rature increase and was observed by the residents of the city of
     Dudinka, near the Polar Circle.
     The paper explained this phenomenon as refraction of the sun's rays
      intercepted by ice crystals at high altitude.

    IRANIAN PLANES       CHASE A UFO

    And UFOs are news once again. In the last weeks the world newspapers
    spilled liters of ink to resume the appearances of strange non-identified
    flying objects in different continents. Yesterday, planes belonging to
    the Iranian air furce chased a flying object which managed to elude them.
    A cable dated at Teheran informs that after midnight the ~ir force guards
    were alerted from the control tower of the Iranian capital's airport. The
    message was concise: "Follow a brilliant flying object that moves at a
    height of some 1,800 meters. Try to identify it and give chase." Immedia-
    tely, the fighters, of American origin, took off and located the object
    which flew away very fast towards the South, at the same time emitting
    green, red and blue lights. During the persecution, the strange object
    moved vertical and horizontally -indistinctly- and disappeared after
    attaining a height of approx. 8,000 meters.
    Information gathered from the local papers states that before climbing to
    that height, from the cabin -from inside, to be more precise- a brilliant
    metallic piece was thrown out, 4 meters diameter. Residents in the South-
    ern sector told the press that one of the air force planes was "at times
    almost beside the UFO", but that the latter managed to escape. There was
    no official report from Iranian authorities. Army troops and Gendarmerie
    patrol the mountains around Teheran in search of the metallic piece
    thrown off -they insist- on purpose by the mysterious visitors.

    COMMOTION IN PRINGLES          =   AN EXTRATERRESTRIAL SHIP MIGHT HAVE LANDED

    Bahra Blanca, 1st (From our correspondent) - A strange event, difficult to
    explain, took place on September 21st last in a field located in the ju-
    risdiction of Coronel Pringles, Buenos Aires Province. However, the event
    considered as of extraordinary importance, was only made known yesterday.
    In fact, the paper "La Nueva Provincia" of this city reports the alleged
    landing of an extraterrestrial vehicle in the aforementioned area.
    The paper's correspondent states that some 6 km West from the center of
    Pringles strange traces appeared on the ground, which are being duly
    analyzed by the National Organization of Space Phenomena's scientific
    team.
    It was learned that the owner of the field, at the time of making a round,
    saw the traces visible from some 200 meters, which were similar to the
    furrows usually made by a plow. The man then observed that this was a
    perfectly defined geometrical figure, as if made by a pressed-down mold.
    It was 10 em deep and the figure was three marks forming a "V" and a
    fourth consisting of two parallel fissures, but shorter, in front of
    which were three circular cylindrical holes 42 em deep. Besides, calcined
    rubble was also found in the area.
    The paper then states that the owner of the field expressed that ovine
    stock did not go near the place. It is also said that the night before
    the discovery of the traces a UFO was seen over the Cnel.Pringles skies,
    a fact confirmed by several responsible witnesses. Concretely, it is
    stated that the form of the traces eliminates the possibility of a
    natural event, the hardness of the earth (that sector has been worked on
    for several years) also eliminates the idea that somebody could have made
    them intentionally, and the terrain's consistency as well as the perfect
    tlssures lead to believe they have been made by full and solid molds
    under the pressure of many tons.

          CHILE: NOW UFOs PREFER VULCANOES

          Talca, Chile, 5  (EFE) - The area Altos de Vilches, at a distance of
          66 km from the Chilean city of Talca, may be a landing place for UFOs.
         This is the general belief of peasants who reside in that region where
         many families already moved due to the appearance of UFOs.
          These emit brilliant lights and come down from three local vulcanoes.
          # THREE VULCANOES
         Men and women said they descend from the vulcanoes "Quizapu","Descabe-
         zado Grande" and "Descabezado Chico", move in the sky for a long time
         and then swiftly disappear. Many local residents did not want to report
         the UFOs' appearan-ce to the authorities to ''avoid being treated as
         lunatics".

        FATIDICAL TRIANGLE

        This Panamanian cargo vessel -the "Sylvia Ossa"- has disappeared a week
        ago with its crew of 37. The last message it sent advised it was enter-
        ing the terrible "Bermuda Triangle" famous for its storms. (A.P. radio-
        photo).

                  GRESHAM, Ore. (UPI) - Santa Claus said he sa• the
             legendary Big Foot.                                        · ·                      JOURNAL & CONSTITUTION
